

  const { error: updateErr } = await db
    .from('profiles')
    .update({ wallet_balance: newBalance })
    .eq('id', matched.customer_id);

  if (updateErr) {
    return errorResponse('Failed to update wallet balance', 500);
  }

  await db.from('wallet_transactions').insert({
    customer_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'credit',
    amount: matched.amount,
    balance_after: newBalance,
    reason: 'Vodafone Cash auto-confirmation',
    reference: matched.transaction_reference ?? `SMS-${Date.now()}`,
  });

  await db.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
    status: 'approved',
    processed_at: now,
    notes: `Auto-approved from SMS: ${message.slice(0, 200)}`,
  }).eq('id', matched.id);

  // Notify customer
  await db.from('notifications').insert({
    user_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'wallet_topup',
    title: 'تم شحن رصيدك تلقائياً ✅',
    body: `تمت إضافة ${matched.amount} Credit إلى محفظتك. رصيدك الآن: ${newBalance} Credit.`,
  });

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, request_id: matched.id, new_balance: newBalance });
});

```

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Wallet, Loader2, MessageCircle, Clock, CheckCircle2, XCircle, AlertCircle, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { CustomerLayout } from '@/components/layouts/CustomerLayout';
import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';
import type { WalletTopupRequest } from '@/types/types';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

const CREDIT_PRICE_EGP = 300;
const SUPPORT_PHONE = '201222692182';

const STATUS_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  pending: 'قيد المراجعة',
  approved: 'تمت الموافقة',
  rejected: 'مرفوض',
};

const STATUS_ICONS = {
  pending: Clock,
  approved: CheckCircle2,
  rejected: XCircle,
};

export default function CustomerTopupRequestPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { profile } = useAuth();
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('1');
  const [senderPhone, setSenderPhone] = useState('');
  const [txRef, setTxRef] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [requests, setRequests] = useState<WalletTopupRequest[]>([]);
  const [fetching, setFetching] = useState(true);

  const creditNum = parseFloat(amount);
  const valid = !isNaN(creditNum) && creditNum >= 0.5;
  const total = valid ? Math.ceil(creditNum) * CREDIT_PRICE_EGP : 0;

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!profile?.id) return;
    setFetching(true);
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('wallet_topup_requests')
      .select('*')
      .eq('customer_id', profile.id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(20);
    setRequests((data ?? []) as WalletTopupRequest[]);
    setFetching(false);
  }, [profile?.id]);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!valid || !profile?.id) return;

    setLoading(true);
    const { error } = await supabase.from('wallet_topup_requests').insert({
      customer_id: profile.id,
      amount: Math.ceil(creditNum),
      sender_phone: senderPhone.trim() || null,
      transaction_reference: txRef.trim() || null,
      payment_method: 'vodafone_cash',
    });

    if (error) {
      toast.error('فشل إرسال طلب الشحن. حاول مرة أخرى.');
    } else {
      toast.success('تم إرسال طلب الشحن. سنتواصل معك عبر الواتساب.');
      setAmount('1');
      setSenderPhone('');
      setTxRef('');
      await load();
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const userName = profile?.email?.split('@')[0] ?? 'عميل';
  const whatsappMessage = encodeURIComponent(
    `السلام عليكم، أنا ${userName}
أريد شراء ${Math.ceil(creditNum || 1)} Credit عبر فودافون كاش
الإجمالي: ${total.toLocaleString('ar-SA')} جنيه مصري`
  );
  const waHref = `https://wa.me/${SUPPORT_PHONE}?text=${whatsappMessage}`;

  return (
    <CustomerLayout>
      <div className="px-4 md:px-6 py-6 space-y-5 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/store/wallet')}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          العودة للمحفظة
        </button>

        <div className="space-y-1">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
            طلب شحن رصيد
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            أرسل طلبك وسنتواصل معك عبر الواتساب لتأكيد الدفع.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Top-up form */}
        <Card className="bg-card border-border">
          <CardHeader className="pb-3">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-semibold">بيانات الشحن</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">عدد الكريدات</Label>
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  min="0.5"
                  step="0.5"
                  value={amount}
                  onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="5"
                  className="text-center text-lg font-semibold"
                />
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">سعر الكريدت: {CREDIT_PRICE_EGP} جنيه مصري</p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/20 border border-border">
                <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">إجمالي الدفع</span>
                <span className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">
                  {total.toLocaleString('ar-SA')} جنيه مصري
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">رقم محمول فودافون كاش (المرسل)</Label>
                <Input
                  type="tel"
                  value={senderPhone}
                  onChange={(e) => setSenderPhone(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="01012345678"
                  dir="ltr"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">رقم العملية أو المرجع (اختياري)</Label>
                <Input
                  value={txRef}
                  onChange={(e) => setTxRef(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="رقم المرجع الظاهر في رسالة التأكيد"
                  dir="ltr"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="p-3 rounded-lg border border-border bg-muted/20 text-xs flex items-start gap-2">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-primary shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <p className="text-muted-foreground">
                  بعد إرسال الطلب ادفع المبلغ عبر فودافون كاش على رقم الواتساب وارسل رسالة التأكيد. سيتم إضافة الرصيد يدوياً أو عند ما يملك الأدمن الهاتف.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={loading || !valid}>
                  {loading && <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 ms-2 animate-spin" />}
                  {loading ? 'جارٍ إرسال الطلب...' : 'إرسال طلب الشحن'}
                </Button>
                <Button asChild variant="outline" className="w-full gap-2">
                  <a href={waHref} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                    <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                    طلب مباشرة عبر واتساب
                  </a>
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {/* Requests history */}
        <Card className="bg-card border-border">
          <CardHeader className="pb-3">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-semibold">طلباتي السابقة</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="p-0">
            {fetching ? (
              <div className="flex justify-center py-8"><Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" /></div>
            ) : requests.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">لا توجد طلبات سابقة</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="divide-y divide-border">
                {requests.map((r) => {
                  const Icon = STATUS_ICONS[r.status as keyof typeof STATUS_ICONS] ?? Clock;
                  return (
                    <div key={r.id} className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground">
                          {r.amount.toFixed(1)} Credit
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {new Date(r.created_at).toLocaleString('ar-SA')}
                        </p>
                        {r.notes && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1 truncate max-w-[200px]">{r.notes}</p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className={`
                        flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium shrink-0
                        ${r.status === 'approved' ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-500' :
                          r.status === 'rejected' ? 'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive' :
                          'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-500'}
                      `}>
                        <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        {STATUS_LABELS[r.status] ?? r.status}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </CustomerLayout>
  );
}

```
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Wallet, Loader2, CheckCircle2, XCircle, ArrowLeft, RefreshCw, Phone } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { AdminLayout } from '@/components/layouts/AdminLayout';
import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
import type { WalletTopupRequest, Profile } from '@/types/types';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

const STATUS_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  pending: 'قيد المراجعة',
  approved: 'تمت الموافقة',
  rejected: 'مرفوض',
};

export default function AdminTopupRequestsPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [requests, setRequests] = useState<WalletTopupRequest[]>([]);
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<Record<string, Profile>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('wallet_topup_requests')
      .select('*, profiles!customer_id(id, email, phone, wallet_balance)')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(50);

    const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as WalletTopupRequest[];
    setRequests(rows);

    const custMap: Record<string, Profile> = {};
    (data ?? []).forEach((row: any) => {
      if (row.profiles) custMap[row.profiles.id] = row.profiles as Profile;
    });
    setCustomers(custMap);
    setLoading(false);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const handleApprove = async (r: WalletTopupRequest) => {
    setProcessing(r.id);
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-wallet-topup', {
        body: {
          customer_id: r.customer_id,
          type: 'credit',
          amount: r.amount,
          reason: r.transaction_reference
            ? `شحن فودافون كاش - رقم المرجع: ${r.transaction_reference}`
            : 'شحن فودافون كاش',
        },
      });

      if (error) {
        toast.error('فشل إضافة الرصيد');
        return;
      }

      await supabase.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
        status: 'approved',
        processed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      }).eq('id', r.id);

      toast.success(`تمت الموافقة على شحن ${r.amount} Credit`);
      await load();
    } finally {
      setProcessing(null);
    }
  };

  const handleReject = async (r: WalletTopupRequest) => {
    setProcessing(r.id);
    await supabase.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
      status: 'rejected',
      processed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      notes: 'Rejected by admin',
    }).eq('id', r.id);
    toast.info('تم رفض الطلب');
    await load();
    setProcessing(null);
  };

  return (
    <AdminLayout>
      <div className="px-4 md:px-6 py-6 space-y-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => navigate('/admin/wallet')}>
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          </Button>
          <div className="space-y-0.5">
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground flex items-center gap-2">
              <Wallet className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
              طلبات شحن الرصيد
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">مراجعة والموافقة على طلبات العملاء</p>
          </div>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="ms-auto gap-1" onClick={load}>
            <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> تحديث
          </Button>
        </div>

        <Card className="bg-card border-border">
          <CardHeader className="pb-3">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-semibold">الطلبات</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="p-0">
            {loading ? (
              <div className="flex justify-center py-8"><Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" /></div>
            ) : requests.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">لا توجد طلبات شحن</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="divide-y divide-border">
                {requests.map((r) => {
                  const c = customers[r.customer_id];
                  return (
                    <div key={r.id} className="p-4 space-y-3">
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground">{c?.email ?? '—'}</p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                            {new Date(r.created_at).toLocaleString('ar-SA')}
                          </p>
                          {r.sender_phone && (
                            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                              <Phone className="w-3 h-3" />
                              {r.sender_phone}
                            </p>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <Badge
                          variant={r.status === 'approved' ? 'default' : r.status === 'rejected' ? 'destructive' : 'secondary'}
                        >
                          {STATUS_LABELS[r.status] ?? r.status}
                        </Badge>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/20 border border-border">
                        <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">الكريدات المطلوبة</span>
                        <span className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">{r.amount.toFixed(1)} Credit</span>
                      </div>

                      {r.transaction_reference && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          رقم المرجع: <span className="font-mono">{r.transaction_reference}</span>
                        </p>
                      )}

                      {r.status === 'pending' && (
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            className="flex-1 gap-1.5"
                            onClick={() => handleApprove(r)}
                            disabled={processing === r.id}
                          >
                            {processing === r.id ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                            موافقة
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant="outline"
                            className="flex-1 gap-1.5"
                            onClick={() => handleReject(r)}
                            disabled={processing === r.id}
                          >
                            <XCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            رفض
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </AdminLayout>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Plug,
  Menu,
  LogOut,
  ChevronRight,
  Shield,
  Users,
  ClipboardList,
  Wallet,
  Settings,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Sheet, SheetContent } from '@/components/ui/sheet';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';

const navItems = [
  { label: 'لوحة التحكم', path: '/', icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { label: 'واجهة المزود (API)', path: '/admin/provider', icon: Plug },
  { label: 'العملاء', path: '/admin/customers', icon: Users },
  { label: 'الطلبات', path: '/admin/orders', icon: ClipboardList },
  { label: 'المحافظ', path: '/admin/wallet', icon: Wallet },
  { label: 'الخدمات', path: '/admin/services', icon: Settings },
];

function NavContent({ onClose }: { onClose?: () => void }) {
  const location = useLocation();
  const { profile, signOut } = useAuth();

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full">
      {/* Brand */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-5 border-b border-sidebar-border">
        <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-primary flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
          <Shield className="w-4 h-4 text-primary-foreground" />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-sidebar-accent-foreground truncate">Nader AI</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground truncate">لوحة الإدارة · Live</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Nav items */}
      <nav className="flex-1 px-2 py-4 space-y-0.5">
        {navItems.map((item) => {
          const active = location.pathname === item.path ||
            (item.path !== '/' && location.pathname.startsWith(item.path));
          return (
            <Link
              key={item.path}
              to={item.path}
              onClick={onClose}
              className={cn(
                'flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded text-sm font-medium transition-colors',
                active
                  ? 'bg-sidebar-accent text-sidebar-accent-foreground border border-primary/20'
                  : 'text-sidebar-foreground hover:bg-sidebar-accent/60 hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground'
              )}
            >
              <item.icon className={cn('w-4 h-4 shrink-0', active ? 'text-primary' : '')} />
              <span className="truncate">{item.label}</span>
              {active && <ChevronRight className="w-3 h-3 me-auto text-primary shrink-0 rotate-180" />}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* User */}
      <div className="px-3 py-4 border-t border-sidebar-border">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 mb-3">
          <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-muted flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground uppercase">
            {profile?.email?.[0] ?? 'A'}
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <p className="text-xs font-medium text-sidebar-accent-foreground truncate">{profile?.email ?? 'admin'}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground capitalize">{profile?.role === 'admin' ? 'مسؤول' : profile?.role ?? 'مسؤول'}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          className="w-full justify-start gap-2 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
          onClick={signOut}
        >
          <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
          تسجيل الخروج
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function AdminLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full bg-background">
      {/* الشريط الجانبي — سطح المكتب */}
      <aside className="hidden md:flex flex-col w-56 shrink-0 bg-sidebar border-l border-sidebar-border">
        <NavContent />
      </aside>

      {/* الشريط الجانبي — الجوال */}
      <Sheet open={mobileOpen} onOpenChange={setMobileOpen}>
        <SheetContent side="right" className="w-56 p-0 bg-sidebar border-l border-sidebar-border">
          <NavContent onClose={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>

      {/* Main content */}
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex flex-col">
        {/* Mobile top bar */}
        <header className="md:hidden flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-b border-border">
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            className="shrink-0"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
          >
            <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />
          </Button>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <Shield className="w-4 h-4 text-primary shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">Nader AI — لوحة الإدارة</span>
          </div>
        </header>

        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

Created File with Text:
```
package com.naderai.smsreader

import android.Manifest
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import androidx.core.app.ActivityCompat
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import com.naderai.smsreader.databinding.ActivityMainBinding

class MainActivity : AppCompatActivity() {

    private lateinit var binding: ActivityMainBinding

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        binding = ActivityMainBinding.inflate(layoutInflater)
        setContentView(binding.root)

        binding.saveButton.setOnClickListener { saveConfig() }
        binding.testButton.setOnClickListener { sendTestWebhook() }

        loadConfig()
        requestSmsPermission()
    }

    private fun requestSmsPermission() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            val permissions = arrayOf(
                Manifest.permission.RECEIVE_SMS,
                Manifest.permission.READ_SMS
            )
            val missing = permissions.filter {
                ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, it) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
            }
            if (missing.isNotEmpty()) {
                ActivityCompat.requestPermissions(this, missing.toTypedArray(), SMS_PERMISSION_CODE)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onRequestPermissionsResult(
        requestCode: Int,
        permissions: Array<out String>,
        grantResults: IntArray
    ) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode, permissions, grantResults)
        if (requestCode == SMS_PERMISSION_CODE) {
            if (grantResults.all { it == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED }) {
                Toast.makeText(this, "تم منح إذن SMS", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            } else {
                Toast.makeText(this, "مطلوب إذن SMS لقراءة رسائل التأكيد", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun saveConfig() {
        val webhookUrl = binding.webhookUrlInput.text.toString().trim()
        val secret = binding.secretInput.text.toString().trim()

        if (webhookUrl.isEmpty() || secret.isEmpty()) {
            Toast.makeText(this, "رابط الـ webhook والسر مطلوبان", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }

        getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, MODE_PRIVATE).edit().apply {
            putString(KEY_WEBHOOK_URL, webhookUrl)
            putString(KEY_SECRET, secret)
            apply()
        }
        Toast.makeText(this, "تم حفظ الإعدادات", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }

    private fun loadConfig() {
        val prefs = getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, MODE_PRIVATE)
        binding.webhookUrlInput.setText(prefs.getString(KEY_WEBHOOK_URL, "https://your-project.supabase.co/functions/v1/wallet-auto-confirm"))
        binding.secretInput.setText(prefs.getString(KEY_SECRET, ""))
    }

    private fun sendTestWebhook() {
        val prefs = getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, MODE_PRIVATE)
        val webhookUrl = prefs.getString(KEY_WEBHOOK_URL, null) ?: return
        val secret = prefs.getString(KEY_SECRET, null) ?: return

        val testBody = mapOf(
            "sender_phone" to "01012345678",
            "message" to "\u0644قد استلمت مبلغ 300.00 جنيه من رقم 01012345678",
            "received_at" to System.currentTimeMillis().toString()
        )

        WebhookSender.send(webhookUrl, secret, testBody) { success, message ->
            runOnUiThread {
                Toast.makeText(this, message, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val PREFS_NAME = "naderai_sms_reader"
        const val KEY_WEBHOOK_URL = "webhook_url"
        const val KEY_SECRET = "webhook_secret"
        private const val SMS_PERMISSION_CODE = 101
    }
}

Created File with Text:
```
package com.naderai.smsreader

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.provider.Telephony
import android.util.Log

class SmsReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {

    override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent) {
        if (intent.action != Telephony.Sms.Intents.SMS_RECEIVED_ACTION) return

        val messages = Telephony.Sms.Intents.getMessagesFromIntent(intent) ?: return

        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(MainActivity.PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val webhookUrl = prefs.getString(MainActivity.KEY_WEBHOOK_URL, null) ?: return
        val secret = prefs.getString(MainActivity.KEY_SECRET, null) ?: return

        for (sms in messages) {
            val messageBody = sms.messageBody ?: continue
            val sender = sms.displayOriginatingAddress ?: "unknown"

            // Only forward Vodafone Cash-like messages
            val keywords = listOf(
                "فودافون كاش", "vodafone cash", "استلمت", "تحويل",
                "مبلغ", "جنيه", "جنيهات", "amount", "received", "transferred"
            )
            val isPayment = keywords.any { messageBody.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) }
            if (!isPayment) {
                Log.d(TAG, "Ignored non-payment SMS from $sender")
                continue
            }

            val payload = mapOf(
                "sender_phone" to sender,
                "message" to messageBody,
                "received_at" to System.currentTimeMillis().toString()
            )

            WebhookSender.send(webhookUrl, secret, payload) { success, msg ->
                Log.d(TAG, "Webhook result: $success, $msg")
            }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "SmsReceiver"
    }
}

```

```
package com.naderai.smsreader

import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import okhttp3.Call
import okhttp3.Callback
import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaType
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody
import okhttp3.Response
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.IOException
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

object WebhookSender {

    private val client = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .build()

    private val jsonMediaType = "application/json; charset=utf-8".toMediaType()

    fun send(
        url: String,
        secret: String,
        body: Map<String, String>,
        onResult: (success: Boolean, message: String) -> Unit
    ) {
        CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO).launch {
            val json = JSONObject(body).toString()
            val request = Request.Builder()
                .url(url)
                .addHeader("X-SMS-Webhook-Secret", secret)
                .addHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                .post(json.toRequestBody(jsonMediaType))
                .build()

            client.newCall(request).enqueue(object : Callback {
                override fun onFailure(call: Call, e: IOException) {
                    onResult(false, "فشل الإرسال: ${e.message}")
                }

                override fun onResponse(call: Call, response: Response) {
                    val responseBody = response.body?.string() ?: ""
                    val success = response.isSuccessful && responseBody.contains("\"ok\":true")
                    onResult(success, if (success) "تم إرسال التأكيد بنجاح" else "استجابة الخادم: ${response.code}")
                }
            })
        }
    }
}

```

Created File with Text:
```
# Vodafone Cash SMS Reader

تطبيق Android بسيط يقرأ رسائل فودافون كاش النصية ويرسل معلوماتها إلى Edge Function الموقع.

## مهمة الأمان

هذا التطبيق يركض على جهاز الأدمن/المالك ويقرأ الرسائل النصية فقط. يجب تأكيد الدفعات يدوياً أو من الواتساب.

## المتطلبات

- Android Studio Hedgehog أو أحدث
- Gradle 8.2+
- Java 17
- هاتف Android مع الـ SMS Receiver

## الإعدادات

1. فتح المشروع في Android Studio.
2. في `MainActivity.kt` املأ الرابط الافتراضي لـ `wallet-auto-confirm`:

```
https://<project-ref>.supabase.co/functions/v1/wallet-auto-confirm
```

3. الصق `SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET` من إعدادات موقعك.
4. انسخ وفعـ الإعدادات.
5. المالك الهاتف يجب أن يكون مفتوحاً ومتصلاً بالإنترنت.

## كيفية العمل

- لما يصل رسالة فودافون كاش لـــ الهاتف، يقوم التطبيق التلقائي بارسال:
  - رقم المرسل
  - نص الرسالة
  - الوقت
- يلوم موقعك فيما إذا كانت البيانات مطابقة لـاحد الطلبات المعلقة، فيما يتم إضافة الرصيد تلقائياً.

## ملاحظات الأمان

- يمكن لأي شخص عنده الهاتف والسر إرسال ويبهوك مزيف. يفضّل تأكيد الدفعات يدوياً بعد الاستلام.
- لا تمسك رسائل البنوك أو رموز البطاقات المساحية.

## ملفات المكونات

- `MainActivity.kt` - واجهة الإعدادات
- `SmsReceiver.kt` - مستقبل الرسائل
- `WebhookSender.kt` - إرسال الويبهوك

```

/**
 * wallet-auto-confirm — receives SMS confirmation from admin Android device
 * POST { sender_phone, message, received_at }
 *
 * Security:
 *  - X-SMS-Webhook-Secret header must match Deno.env SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET
 *  - Matches pending wallet_topup_request by sender phone + amount
 *  - Credits customer atomically, marks request approved
 */
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  // Look for numbers like 100, 100.00, 1,000, etc. preceded/followed by currency hints

COMMAND:
cd /workspace/app-dpgpkghtekg1 && cat > supabase/functions/wallet-auto-confirm2/index.ts << 'EOF'
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const matches = [
    ...text.matchAll(/(?:amount|egp)\s*([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/gi),
    ...text.matchAll(/([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})\s*(?:egp)/gi),
  ];
  for (const m of matches) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  const fallback = text.match(/[\d,]+\.?\d{0,2}/);
  if (fallback) {
    const n = parseFloat(fallback[0].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  const payload = await req.json();
  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  if (!message) return errorResponse('Message required', 400);

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '') ?? null;
  const amount = extractAmount(message);
  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, senderPhone, amount });
});
EOF

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const matches = [
    ...text.matchAll(/(?:مبلغ|amount|egp|جنيه|ج\.?\s*)\s*([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/gi),
    ...text.matchAll(/([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})\s*(?:جنيه|egp|ج\.?)/gi),
  ];
  for (const m of matches) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  const payload = await req.json();
  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  if (!message) return errorResponse('Message required', 400);

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '') ?? null;
  const amount = extractAmount(message);
  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, senderPhone, amount });
});
EOF

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const matches = [
    ...text.matchAll(/(?:مبلغ|amount|egp)\s*([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/gi),
  ];
  for (const m of matches) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  const payload = await req.json();
  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  if (!message) return errorResponse('Message required', 400);

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '') ?? null;
  const amount = extractAmount(message);
  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, message: 'تم شحن رصيدك ✅', senderPhone, amount });
});

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
});
EOF

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, count: request?.length ?? 0 });
});
EOF

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const matches = [
    ...text.matchAll(/(?:مبلغ|amount|egp|جنيه|ج\.?\s*)\s*([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/gi),
    ...text.matchAll(/([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})\s*(?:جنيه|egp|ج\.?)/gi),
  ];
  for (const m of matches) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  const fallback = text.match(/[\d,]+\.?\d{0,2}/);
  if (fallback) {
    const n = parseFloat(fallback[0].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

function extractSenderPhone(text: string): string | null {
  const m = text.match(/(?:\+?20)?\s*0?1\d{9}/);
  return m ? normalizeEgyptianPhone(m[0]) : null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  if (!message) return errorResponse('Message required', 400);

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '') ?? extractSenderPhone(message);
  const amount = extractAmount(message);

  if (!senderPhone || !amount) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Could not parse sender phone or amount', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  const matched = (request ?? []).find((r) => {
    const reqPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(r.sender_phone ?? '');
    return reqPhone === senderPhone && Math.abs(r.amount - amount) < 0.5;
  });

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, matched_id: matched?.id ?? null });
});
EOF

</action>

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const matches = [
    ...text.matchAll(/(?:مبلغ|amount|egp|جنيه|ج\.?\s*)\s*([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/gi),
    ...text.matchAll(/([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})\s*(?:جنيه|egp|ج\.?)/gi),
  ];
  for (const m of matches) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  const fallback = text.match(/[\d,]+\.?\d{0,2}/);
  if (fallback) {
    const n = parseFloat(fallback[0].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

function extractSenderPhone(text: string): string | null {
  const m = text.match(/(?:\+?20)?\s*0?1\d{9}/);
  return m ? normalizeEgyptianPhone(m[0]) : null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  if (!message) return errorResponse('Message required', 400);

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '') ?? extractSenderPhone(message);
  const amount = extractAmount(message);

  if (!senderPhone || !amount) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Could not parse sender phone or amount', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  const matched = (request ?? []).find((r) => {
    const reqPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(r.sender_phone ?? '');
    return reqPhone === senderPhone && Math.abs(r.amount - amount) < 0.5;
  });

  if (!matched) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'No matching pending request', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const { data: profile } = await db
    .from('profiles')
    .select('wallet_balance')
    .eq('id', matched.customer_id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (!profile) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Customer profile not found' });
  }

  const newBalance = (profile.wallet_balance ?? 0) + matched.amount;
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  const { error: updateErr } = await db
    .from('profiles')
    .update({ wallet_balance: newBalance })
    .eq('id', matched.customer_id);

  if (updateErr) {
    return errorResponse('Failed to update wallet balance', 500);
  }

  await db.from('wallet_transactions').insert({
    customer_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'credit',
    amount: matched.amount,
    balance_after: newBalance,
    reason: 'Vodafone Cash auto-confirmation',
    reference: matched.transaction_reference ?? `SMS-${Date.now()}`,
  });

  await db.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
    status: 'approved',
    processed_at: now,
    notes: `Auto-approved from SMS: ${message.slice(0, 200)}`,
  }).eq('id', matched.id);

  await db.from('notifications').insert({
    user_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'wallet_topup',
    title: 'تم شحن رصيدك تلقائياً',
    body: `تمت إضافة ${matched.amount} Credit إلى محفظتك. رصيدك الآن: ${newBalance} Credit.`,
  });

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, request_id: matched.id, new_balance: newBalance });
});
EOF

</action>

COMMAND:
cd /workspace/app-dpgpkghtekg1 && cat > supabase/functions/wallet-auto-confirm2/index.ts << 'EOF'
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const matches = [
    ...text.matchAll(/(?:مبلغ|amount|egp|جنيه|ج\.?\s*)\s*([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/gi),
    ...text.matchAll(/([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})\s*(?:جنيه|egp|ج\.?)/gi),
  ];
  for (const m of matches) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  const fallback = text.match(/[\d,]+\.?\d{0,2}/);
  if (fallback) {
    const n = parseFloat(fallback[0].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

function extractSenderPhone(text: string): string | null {
  const m = text.match(/(?:\+?20)?\s*0?1\d{9}/);
  return m ? normalizeEgyptianPhone(m[0]) : null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  if (!message) return errorResponse('Message required', 400);

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '') ?? extractSenderPhone(message);
  const amount = extractAmount(message);

  if (!senderPhone || !amount) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Could not parse sender phone or amount', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  const matched = (request ?? []).find((r) => {
    const reqPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(r.sender_phone ?? '');
    return reqPhone === senderPhone && Math.abs(r.amount - amount) < 0.5;
  });

  if (!matched) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'No matching pending request', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const { data: profile } = await db
    .from('profiles')
    .select('wallet_balance')
    .eq('id', matched.customer_id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (!profile) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Customer profile not found' });
  }

  const newBalance = (profile.wallet_balance ?? 0) + matched.amount;
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  const { error: updateErr } = await db
    .from('profiles')
    .update({ wallet_balance: newBalance })
    .eq('id', matched.customer_id);

  if (updateErr) {
    return errorResponse('Failed to update wallet balance', 500);
  }

  await db.from('wallet_transactions').insert({
    customer_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'credit',
    amount: matched.amount,
    balance_after: newBalance,
    reason: 'Vodafone Cash auto-confirmation',
    reference: matched.transaction_reference ?? `SMS-${Date.now()}`,
  });

  await db.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
    status: 'approved',
    processed_at: now,
    notes: `Auto-approved from SMS: ${message.slice(0, 200)}`,
  }).eq('id', matched.id);

  await db.from('notifications').insert({
    user_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'wallet_topup',
    title: 'Wallet auto top-up',
    body: `Added ${matched.amount} Credit. New balance: ${newBalance} Credit.`,
  });

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, request_id: matched.id, new_balance: newBalance });
});
EOF

  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const re1 = /(?:مبلغ|amount|egp|جنيه|ج\.?\s*)\s*([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/gi;
  let m1;
  while ((m1 = re1.exec(text)) !== null) {
    const n = parseFloat(m1[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  const re2 = /([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})\s*(?:جنيه|egp|ج\.?)/gi;
  let m2;
  while ((m2 = re2.exec(text)) !== null) {
    const n = parseFloat(m2[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  const fallback = text.match(/[\d,]+\.?\d{0,2}/);
  if (fallback) {
    const n = parseFloat(fallback[0].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

function extractSenderPhone(text: string): string | null {
  const m = text.match(/(?:\+?20)?\s*0?1\d{9}/);
  return m ? normalizeEgyptianPhone(m[0]) : null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  if (!message) return errorResponse('Message required', 400);

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '') ?? extractSenderPhone(message);
  const amount = extractAmount(message);

  if (!senderPhone || !amount) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Could not parse sender phone or amount', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  const matched = (request ?? []).find((r) => {
    const reqPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(r.sender_phone ?? '');
    return reqPhone === senderPhone && Math.abs(r.amount - amount) < 0.5;
  });

  if (!matched) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'No matching pending request', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const { data: profile } = await db
    .from('profiles')
    .select('wallet_balance')
    .eq('id', matched.customer_id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (!profile) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Customer profile not found' });
  }

  const newBalance = (profile.wallet_balance ?? 0) + matched.amount;
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  const { error: updateErr } = await db
    .from('profiles')
    .update({ wallet_balance: newBalance })
    .eq('id', matched.customer_id);

  if (updateErr) {
    return errorResponse('Failed to update wallet balance', 500);
  }

  await db.from('wallet_transactions').insert({
    customer_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'credit',
    amount: matched.amount,
    balance_after: newBalance,
    reason: 'Vodafone Cash auto-confirmation',
    reference: matched.transaction_reference ?? `SMS-${Date.now()}`,
  });

  await db.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
    status: 'approved',
    processed_at: now,
    notes: `Auto-approved from SMS: ${message.slice(0, 200)}`,
  }).eq('id', matched.id);

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, request_id: matched.id, new_balance: newBalance });
});
EOF

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const re = /([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/;
  const m = text.match(re);
  if (m) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

    payload = await req.json();

  );

    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, count: (request ?? []).length });
});

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: profile } = await db.from('profiles').select('wallet_balance').eq('id', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000').maybeSingle();
  if (profile) {
    await db.from('profiles').update({ wallet_balance: 0 }).eq('id', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000');
  }

  await db.from('wallet_transactions').insert({
    customer_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
    type: 'credit',
    amount: 1,
    balance_after: 1,
    reason: 'test',
    reference: 'test',
  });

  await db.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
    status: 'approved',
    processed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    notes: 'Auto-approved',
  }).eq('id', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000');

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
});

COMMAND:
cd /workspace/app-dpgpkghtekg1 && cat > supabase/functions/wallet-sms-confirm/index.ts << 'EOF'
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const re = /([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/;
  const m = text.match(re);
  if (m) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '') ?? null;
  const amount = extractAmount(message);

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  const matched = (request ?? []).find((r) => {
    const reqPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(r.sender_phone ?? '');
    return reqPhone === senderPhone && Math.abs(r.amount - amount) < 0.5;
  });

  if (!matched) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'No matching pending request', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const { data: profile } = await db.from('profiles').select('wallet_balance').eq('id', matched.customer_id).maybeSingle();
  if (!profile) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Customer profile not found' });
  }

  const newBalance = (profile.wallet_balance ?? 0) + matched.amount;
  await db.from('profiles').update({ wallet_balance: newBalance }).eq('id', matched.customer_id);

  await db.from('wallet_transactions').insert({
    customer_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'credit',
    amount: matched.amount,
    balance_after: newBalance,
    reason: 'Vodafone Cash auto-confirmation',
    reference: matched.transaction_reference ?? `SMS-${Date.now()}`,
  });

  await db.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
    status: 'approved',
    processed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    notes: `Auto-approved from SMS: ${message.slice(0, 200)}`,
  }).eq('id', matched.id);

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, request_id: matched.id, new_balance: newBalance });
});
EOF

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;

  const db = createClient(

  }

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  amount?: number;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '');
  const amount = payload.amount;
  if (!senderPhone || !amount) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'sender_phone and amount are required' });
  }

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  const matched = (request ?? []).find((r) => {
    const reqPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(r.sender_phone ?? '');
    return reqPhone === senderPhone && Math.abs(r.amount - amount) < 0.5;
  });

  if (!matched) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'No matching pending request', sender_phone, amount });
  }

  const { data: profile } = await db.from('profiles').select('wallet_balance').eq('id', matched.customer_id).maybeSingle();
  if (!profile) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Customer profile not found' });
  }

  const newBalance = (profile.wallet_balance ?? 0) + matched.amount;
  const { error: updateErr } = await db.from('profiles').update({ wallet_balance: newBalance }).eq('id', matched.customer_id);
  if (updateErr) {
    return errorResponse('Failed to update wallet balance', 500);
  }

  await db.from('wallet_transactions').insert({
    customer_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'credit',
    amount: matched.amount,
    balance_after: newBalance,
    reason: 'Vodafone Cash auto-confirmation',
    reference: matched.transaction_reference ?? `SMS-${Date.now()}`,
  });

  await db.from('wallet_topup_requests').update({
    status: 'approved',
    processed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    notes: `Auto-approved from SMS sender ${sender_phone}`,
  }).eq('id', matched.id);

  await db.from('notifications').insert({
    user_id: matched.customer_id,
    type: 'wallet_topup',
    title: 'تم شحن رصيدك تلقائياً',
    body: `تمت إضافة ${matched.amount} Credit إلى محفظتك. رصيدك الآن: ${newBalance} Credit.`,
  });

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, request_id: matched.id, new_balance: newBalance });
});
EOF

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  const matched = (request ?? []).find((r) => {
    const reqPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(r.sender_phone ?? '');
    return reqPhone === senderPhone && Math.abs(r.amount - amount) < 0.5;
  });

  if (!matched) return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'no match' });

  const { data: profile } = await db.from('profiles').select('wallet_balance').eq('id', matched.customer_id).maybeSingle();
  if (!profile) return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'profile not found' });

  const newBalance = (profile.wallet_balance ?? 0) + matched.amount;
  await db.from('profiles').update({ wallet_balance: newBalance }).eq('id', matched.customer_id);

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, new_balance: newBalance });
});

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  amount?: number;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  return null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '');
  const amount = payload.amount;
  if (!senderPhone || !amount) return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'missing' });

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data: request } = await db
    .from('wallet_topup_requests')
    .select('id, customer_id, amount, status, sender_phone, transaction_reference')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);

  const matched = (request ?? []).find((r) => {
    const reqPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(r.sender_phone ?? '');
    return reqPhone === senderPhone && Math.abs(r.amount - amount) < 0.5;
  });

  if (!matched) return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'no match' });

  const { data: profile } = await db.from('profiles').select('wallet_balance').eq('id', matched.customer_id).maybeSingle();
  if (!profile) return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'profile not found' });

  const newBalance = (profile.wallet_balance ?? 0) + matched.amount;
  await db.from('profiles').update({ wallet_balance: newBalance }).eq('id', matched.customer_id);

  return jsonResponse({ ok: true, new_balance: newBalance });
});
EOF

COMMAND:
cd /workspace/app-dpgpkghtekg1 && mkdir -p supabase/functions/wallet-auto-confirm && cat > supabase/functions/wallet-auto-confirm/index.ts << 'EOF'
import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2';
import { handleCors, jsonResponse, errorResponse } from '../_shared/cors.ts';

interface SmsPayload {
  sender_phone?: string;
  amount?: number;
  message?: string;
  received_at?: string;
}

function normalizeEgyptianPhone(raw: string): string | null {
  const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (digits.length === 10 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.startsWith('01')) return digits;
  if (digits.length === 12 && digits.startsWith('20') && digits[2] === '1') return digits.slice(2);
  if (digits.length === 13 && digits.startsWith('+20') && digits[3] === '1') return digits.slice(3);
  if (digits.length === 9 && digits.startsWith('1')) return '0' + digits;
  return null;
}

function extractAmount(text: string): number | null {
  const re = /([\d,]+\.?\d{0,2})/;
  const m = text.match(re);
  if (m) {
    const n = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
  }
  return null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  const cors = handleCors(req);
  if (cors) return cors;
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return errorResponse('Method not allowed', 405);

  const secret = req.headers.get('X-SMS-Webhook-Secret');
  const expected = Deno.env.get('SMS_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
  if (!expected || secret !== expected) return errorResponse('Invalid secret', 401);

  let payload: SmsPayload;
  try {
    payload = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return errorResponse('Invalid JSON', 400);
  }

  const message = (payload.message ?? '').trim();
  let senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(payload.sender_phone ?? '');
  let amount = payload.amount;

  if (!senderPhone && message) {
    const m = message.match(/(?:\+?20)?\s*0?1\d{9}/);
    if (m) senderPhone = normalizeEgyptianPhone(m[0]);
  }

  if (!amount && message) {
    amount = extractAmount(message);
  }

  if (!senderPhone || !amount) {
    return jsonResponse({ ok: false, reason: 'Could not parse sender_phone or amount', sender_phone: senderPhone, amount });
  }

  const db = createClient(
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
    Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!,
    { auth: { persistSession: false } },
  );

  const { data, error } = await db.rpc('auto_confirm_wallet_topup', {
    p_sender_phone: senderPhone,
    p_amount: amount,
  });

  if (error) {
    return errorResponse(error.message, 500);
  }

  return jsonResponse(data);
});
EOF

     1 package com.naderai.smsreader
     2 
     3 import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
     4 import android.content.Context
     5 import android.content.Intent
     6 import android.provider.Telephony
     7 import android.util.Log
     8 
     9 class SmsReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {
    10 
    11     override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent) {
    12         if (intent.action != Telephony.Sms.Intents.SMS_RECEIVED_ACTION) return
    13 
    14         val messages = Telephony.Sms.Intents.getMessagesFromIntent(intent) ?: return
    15 
    16         val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(MainActivity.PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    17         val webhookUrl = prefs.getString(MainActivity.KEY_WEBHOOK_URL, null) ?: return
    18         val secret = prefs.getString(MainActivity.KEY_SECRET, null) ?: return
    19 
    20         for (sms in messages) {
    21             val messageBody = sms.messageBody ?: continue
    22             val sender = sms.displayOriginatingAddress ?: "unknown"
    23 
    24             // Only forward Vodafone Cash-like messages
    25             val keywords = listOf(
    26                 "فودافون كاش", "vodafone cash", "استلمت", "تحويل",
    27                 "مبلغ", "جنيه", "جنيهات", "amount", "received", "transferred"
    28             )
    29             val isPayment = keywords.any { messageBody.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) }
    30             if (!isPayment) {
    31                 Log.d(TAG, "Ignored non-payment SMS from $sender")
    32                 continue
    33             }
    34 
    35             val payload = mapOf(
    36                 "sender_phone" to sender,
    37                 "message" to messageBody,
    38                 "received_at" to System.currentTimeMillis().toString()
    39             )
    40 
    41             WebhookSender.send(webhookUrl, secret, payload) { success, msg ->
    42                 Log.d(TAG, "Webhook result: $success, $msg")
    43             }
    44         }
    45     }
    46 
    47     companion object {
    48         private const val TAG = "SmsReceiver"
    49     }
    50 }

            val amount = parseAmount(messageBody)
            if (amount == null) {
                Log.d(TAG, "Could not parse amount from SMS: $messageBody")
            }

            val payload = mapOf(

                "amount" to (amount?.toString() ?: ""),
                "message" to messageBody,
                "received_at" to System.currentTimeMillis().toString()
            )

            WebhookSender.send(webhookUrl, secret, payload) { success, msg ->
                Log.d(TAG, "Webhook result: $success, $msg")
            }
        }
    }

    private fun parseAmount(text: String): Double? {
        // Look for Arabic / English currency hints first
        val hintRegex = Regex("(?:مبلغ|amount|egp|جنيه|ج\\.?)\\s*([\\d,]+\\.?\\d{0,2})", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
        val hintMatch = hintRegex.find(text)
        if (hintMatch != null) {
            val n = hintMatch.groupValues[1].replace(",", "").toDoubleOrNull()
            if (n != null && n > 0) return n
        }
        // Fallback to any number
        val fallback = Regex("([\\d,]+\\.?\\d{0,2})").find(text)
        if (fallback != null) {
            val n = fallback.groupValues[1].replace(",", "").toDoubleOrNull()
            if (n != null && n > 0) return n
        }
        return null
    }

export const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS',
};

export function handleCors(req: Request): Response | null {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  }
  return null;
}

export function jsonResponse(data: unknown, status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    status,
    headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  });
}

export function errorResponse(message: string, status = 500): Response {
  return jsonResponse({ error: message }, status);
}

     3 import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
     4 import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
     5 import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

     8 import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaType
     9 import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
    10 import okhttp3.Request
    11 import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody
    12 import okhttp3.Response
    13 import org.json.JSONObject
    14 import java.io.IOException
    15 import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
    16 
    17 object WebhookSender {
    18 
    19     private val client = OkHttpClient.Builder()
    20         .connectTimeout(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
    21         .readTimeout(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
    22         .build()
    23 
    24     private val jsonMediaType = "application/json; charset=utf-8".toMediaType()
    25 
    26     fun send(
    27         url: String,
    28         secret: String,
    29         body: Map<String, String>,
    30         onResult: (success: Boolean, message: String) -> Unit
    31     ) {
    32         CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO).launch {
    33             val json = JSONObject(body).toString()
    34             val request = Request.Builder()
    35                 .url(url)
    36                 .addHeader("X-SMS-Webhook-Secret", secret)
    37                 .addHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
    38                 .post(json.toRequestBody(jsonMediaType))
    39                 .build()
    40 
    41             client.newCall(request).enqueue(object : Callback {
    42                 override fun onFailure(call: Call, e: IOException) {
    43                     onResult(false, "فشل الإرسال: ${e.message}")
    44                 }
    45 
    46                 override fun onResponse(call: Call, response: Response) {
    47                     val responseBody = response.body?.string() ?: ""
    48                     val success = response.isSuccessful && responseBody.contains("\"ok\":true")
    49                     onResult(success, if (success) "تم إرسال التأكيد بنجاح" else "استجابة الخادم: ${response.code}")
    50                 }
    51             })
    52         }
    53     }
    54 }
